# Changelog

All notable changes to this project are documented in this file.

The format is based on [Keep a Changelog](https://keepachangelog.com/en/1.1.0/).
Versioning: the release line was reset on 2026-10-21 — releases are 0.0.x and
increment in patch order (0.0.1, then 0.0.2, then 0.0.3, and so on) until
otherwise specified; the release tag must always equal package.json's version.

Repo conventions: measured claims cite their receipts (`docs/testing-strategy.md`, `benchmarks/memory-eval/*/RESULTS.md`). Changes to agent behavior — prompts, default models, routing, guard rules, hooks, memory injection — are filed under **Agent Behavior**; the policy lives in [docs/BEHAVIOR.md](docs/BEHAVIOR.md).

## [0.0.12] - 2026-10-24

### Added

- **release: the pending-plates round ships with the Windows release-gate fixes — v0.0.12 supersedes the failed v0.0.11 tag** — the v0.0.11 tag's release run failed its windows-latest verification on the `headless-guards.test.ts` `mkdtemp('/tmp/...')` ENOENT (the CI-round test file had never faced a Windows runner; the fix landed on main minutes after the tag was cut, too late for the tag's lineage). Rather than move a dead tag, this release cuts v0.0.12 from the merged main, which carries BOTH the pending-plates round (every capability filmed, no duplicate faces — the dex's twenty-two entries all captured, the last honest-empty stages retired) AND the complete Windows release-gate fix line: the resume-test fixture now serializes its JSON properly (raw `D:\` interpolation was invalid JSON on Windows), and the headless-guards schema block uses the suite's `TEST_TMP` redirect. The v0.0.10 release below already shipped the DOX v3 round with these fixes verified by its own green 3-OS matrix; this release adds the pending-plates content on top. Verified: the merged main's full CI green (CI/CodeQL/Scorecard on a597dc4), the v0.0.10 matrix as the Windows proof of the fix line, and this release's own 3-OS verification.

## [0.0.11] - 2026-10-24

### Added

- **landing: the pending-plates round — every capability filmed, no duplicate faces** — the dex's ten "no capture yet" plates (what a visitor actually met: four of the first seven entries showed an empty stage, which is what "the videos don't work and not all videos are present" looked like on the live page) are closed with real captured footage, and the three lanes whose posters shipped byte-identical to other lanes' boot frames are re-captured with pane-true beats. **Ten new lanes**, every one driven through the ttyd capture harness against the live stack (bridge + engine, agnes-3.0-flash through the Agnes AI upstream), pane-truth + pixel + VLM audited, frame-scanned, video-sampled, and md5-distinct: **websearch** (web_search visibly cascading the keyless chain, the answer with its cited source — a stable-forever question so the lane never rots), **mcp** (a real MCP stdio server — `scripts/tui-capture/fixtures/mini-mcp-dice.mjs`, actual newline-delimited JSON-RPC initialize/tools/list/tools/call, zero dependencies — added via `unown mcp add`, its `dice/roll` tool called live in-session), **lsp** (/repomap's graph-ranked map, then a cross-tree rename while typescript-language-server's diagnostics ride every edit's tool result), **parity-round** (the 2026 consensus round in one live session: the /output-style listing and set, the /btw overlay answering from context, the /insights toast — its always-visible `window:` tail is the anchor; the report toast is taller than the pane, so its section headers scroll away — and the auto-titled session proven by /name), **harness** (the Learning Lab screen against real engine cycles driven through the engine API; the dashboard opens with focus on the section sidebar, so the lane Tabs into the content list before Enter), **router** (the real job table — dreams, harness, automations, one busy-mutex), **routines** (a real CLI-created routine — a daily 09:00 morning brief, read-only — whose manual run SUCCEEDED on record and whose scheduled 09:00 run fired live during the capture window), **skills** (the Skills screen after the self-improvement loop genuinely shipped a skill: `lint-changed-files`, dream-minted, Biome auto-fixes scoped to changed pi-repo files — plus the loop's later mints), **goals** (a real /goal run: the objective riding the next message's prompt, todo-tracked work, the completion gate closing on the FOOTER STATE `goal: ✓ complete` — prose anchors false-match the model's own text — with both kinds of cited evidence and the /goal status record), and **split-view** — FILMED (the dex copy said "Stills only — this lane ships no movie"; the movie supersedes the PTY stills cut through the audited ttyd pipeline: /split mounts the band + dock, a live turn fills the Thinking and Tool Usage panels while the files dock carries the edit diff). **Three lanes re-captured honest**: review's poster now shows the actual uncommitted diff read into the pane (`!git diff` before the still — the fixture's fix lived in the filesystem, and a plain boot frame is byte-identical to every other lane's; its 04 beat is the user's own `ls` of the saved review artifact, because the verdict and the Saved-to path land in ONE result block and no two stills could differ), subagent-explore's poster is the typed exploration ask (its boot frame was byte-identical to fleet's), and hero-bugfix's tail is the finished green turn plus the user's flake-guard `!bun test` re-run (agnes-3.0-flash lands the bash re-run and a short final answer inside one still's settle window, so the old 05/06 shipped byte-identical twice); plan-mode's poster is re-pointed at its own numbered-plan still — the face of its story. The dex now ships **22 movies + the browser-subagent stills, one deliberate pending plate** (№ 010 Parity, the checklist), and the derived default pane is № 001 Websearch. **Harness hardening found live**: tmux does not know the key name `Esc` — `send-keys` typed the LITERAL STRING into modal overlays (verified via `cat -v`), so `driver.js` gains a `KEY_ALIASES` map (Esc→Escape and friends) — the /btw overlay could never be dismissed and silently ate the next command's typing; `run_lane.sh` runs one lane end to end (prep → time-boxed driver → assemble → all three audit gates → an md5-distinctness check) and wipes the lane dir first (stale stills from renamed beats failed audits with "no pane dump found"); `pipeline-a..e.sh` chain the rounds; `skills-prep.sh` drives the real mint→run→ship loop; the goals lane anchors mid-work on the FILESYSTEM (git status) instead of pane text (boot-context prose false-matched `read`), and the /btw-sensitive captures run against a budget-throttled engine (`PI_ENGINE_MAX_CALLS_HOUR=1`) so the bridge's background lane answers the side-channel promptly — the /insights timeout was the dream queue, not the command. **New gates, so "the video doesn't work" can never again be discovered by a visitor**: `dex-media.test.tsx` (every media path the rendered DOM references exists under `public/`, every `<img>` with declared intrinsic dims matches the PNG's real IHDR size — the split-view stills shipped 1792×1042 while the dex declared 2214×1369 — every referenced mp4 has faststart, every webm the EBML magic, and **no two entries ship byte-identical posters**); `e2e/dex-video.spec.ts` (the plays-like-a-real-user gate: selecting each filmed entry drives its video to readyState 4, presents frames via `requestVideoFrameCallback`, advances `currentTime`, zero MediaError, zero failed media fetches; the pending plate stays honest with no mounted media). Also in the round: the changelog rail's `MAX_LISTED` 5→3 (the rail's own rule is "the fortieth release costs the page nothing", but listed titles cost scroll-budget words the sideways axis cannot hide — the eight-stop rail alone had grown to 585 of them; the page measured 3220 words against the 3200 gate on clean HEAD), `vercel.json` sends `/landing/*` with `s-maxage=31536000` (every media fetch was `max-age=0, must-revalidate` — repeat visits re-downloaded the audited captures), and the stale-number doc drift (tui/README № 017→018, the dex-data behavior ranges, the creature-test count, the AGENTS.md OG-set sentence) is corrected. Verified: 103 unit tests (the four media gates included), typecheck + lint unchanged, production build + standalone smoke green, full e2e 9/9 — the decode gate walks all 22 movies and every one reaches HAVE_ENOUGH_DATA and presents frames; the real-user browser audit shows 21/22 filmed, all playing, zero console errors, zero failed requests.

## [0.0.10] - 2026-10-24

### Added

- **parity: the trust round — in-session permission modes + additional working directories** — the two most user-visible trust gaps from the 2026-10-24 reverse-engineering sweep (four parallel research agents over Claude Code, Gemini CLI, Codex, OpenCode, Crush, Amp, Aider, Goose), both verified live. **`/permissions mode <default|acceptEdits|bypassPermissions|reset>`**: the session-scoped mode override (Claude Code's Shift+Tab lane, Gemini CLI's `--approval-mode`, goose `/mode`, Crush ctrl+y, OpenCode `--auto` — every competitor ships an in-session switch; Unown's modes were config-file only). The config files are NEVER written; the override survives config reloads; `UNOWN_STRICT_HOOKS=1` refuses bypass exactly like a config declaration; `/permissions status` reports `modeSource` (config|session). **`--permission-mode <m>`**: the launch twin (print + TUI; usage error exits 2). **`--add-dir <path>`** (repeatable) **+ `/add-dir <path>|list|clear`**: additional working directories (Claude Code `--add-dir` / Gemini `--include-directories` parity — the write jail was hard-coded to project+tmp, so cross-repo work was impossible). `write-roots.ts` owns the registry: user-initiated roots, realpath-resolved at add time, degenerate roots refused, dedupe, session scope only; `terminalPathOk` consults the roots after project+tmp (write/edit tools); `bashRedirectEscape`'s system-tree exemption covers them; `instruction-guard.ts` applies its FULL SA-B2 deny set relative to every added root — an added root's `AGENTS.md` / `.unown/hooks.json` / `.claude/settings.json` stay in-session-unwritable, and devices and `.ssh` are never exempt: the instruction-set gate stays final, root or no root. Tests: permissions-mode (19), write-roots (15), instruction-guard still 67, permissions 86, parse-args +4; the full suite 4154 pass / 0 fail. Live verification: `scripts/tui-capture/pty_capture_trust.py` — 6/6 PASS (launch flags land, status shows the override + dirs, live mode switch, add-dir list/clear, mode reset), plus the e2e BLOCKED/WROTE matrix: a write tool call to `../shared` BLOCKED without the grant, WROTE with `--add-dir ../shared` (real model, real tool call, receipts-verified). Docs: [permissions](docs/permissions.md) (modes + add-dir sections), [usage](docs/usage.md) (commands + trust flags), root + self-learning AGENTS.md contracts.
- **parity: the flow round — --continue/--resume launch + project settings** — the flow-round consensus gaps (Claude Code `claude -c`/`-r`, Codex resume, Crush `--continue`, OpenCode `-c`, plus the project-config layers of Claude Code/Gemini/Codex), each verified live. **`unown -c` / `--continue`**: reopen this directory's latest session — `resume.ts` scans BOTH stores (`~/.unown/agent/sessions` + the project's `.unown/sessions` imports), newest-first, header cwd realpath-compared. **`unown --resume <id|name|path>` / `-r`**: id full-or-prefix (from the filename, no file reads), name substring over the latest `session_info` (auto-titles and `/name` both feed it, newest wins, ambiguous matches list the five newest candidates instead of guessing), or exact file path (the `--session` spelling). Failures exit 1 with actionable messages; the landing notice goes to stderr so `--output-format json`'s stdout contract stays clean. Works in TUI and print mode (with `-p`: resume-and-run). **`.unown/settings.json`** (project settings layer, trust-gated like every project config): `model` / `provider` / `effort` / `permissionMode` / `addDirs` pin per-project session defaults; precedence CLI > project > user defaults — a flag the command line set is never overwritten; unknown keys warn and drop, never fatal; `/project-settings [status|trust]` in-session. Verified live: an untrusted file's keys are ignored with one boot notice; trusted, the model pin routes the session (measured: the session's `model_change` entry landed on agnes-2.5-flash). FIXED on the way (found live while verifying): the vendored pi footer and `/session` stats crashed on assistant messages without a usage block — imported sessions (the v0.0.8 cross-agent import lane writes exactly that shape) could never resume in the TUI; both call sites now guard, dist rebuilt (crash receipt: "undefined is not an object (evaluating 'usage.input')" from footer.js:147). Tests: `resume.test.ts` (17: both stores, id/name/path resolution, ambiguity, trust gate, parse validation), parse-args +4; the full self-learning suite 4260 pass / 0 fail. Live verification: `scripts/tui-capture/pty_capture_flow.py` — 4/4 PASS, plus CLI smokes: `-c` recalled the prior turn's answer, `--resume <id-prefix>` answered "3" (the real message count), `--resume <name>` recalled the crafted magic word, the ambiguous-id error listed both candidates. Docs: [usage](docs/usage.md) (resume + project-settings paragraphs, command rows), [configuration](docs/configuration.md) (the project settings section), root + self-learning AGENTS.md contracts.
- **parity: the CI round — --max-turns + --output-schema headless hardening** — the CI-round consensus gaps (Claude Code `--max-turns`/`--json-schema`, Gemini CLI `--max-turns` + documented exit codes, Codex `exec --output-schema`), each verified live. **`unown -p --max-turns N`**: the bounded headless run. A turn is ONE MODEL CALL — pi fires `message_start` per assistant message, while `agent_start`/`agent_end` bracket the whole agent LOOP for one user prompt (the first implementation counted loops and capped nothing on a multi-tool task — found live, fixed, and pinned in tests). The (N+1)th model call aborts through pi's own `ctx.abort` (the Ctrl-C path: disposal, session notes and exit semantics behave as an interrupted run); the answer produced so far stands. The JSON envelope gains `capped: true` and a `num_turns` that counts COMPLETED turns (the aborted partial's `message_end` is subtracted); a capped run WITH an answer reports subtype success — the cap is a controlled stop, not an execution error. Verified live: `--max-turns 1` on a two-turn task -> `num_turns 1, capped true`; `--max-turns 2` -> `num_turns 2, capped false`, subtype success, the real answer. **`unown -p --output-schema <file>`**: the final answer must parse as JSON (ONE wrapping \u0060\u0060\u0060json fence is stripped — the LLM padding lane) and validate against the JSON Schema (`jsonschema.ts`, the task tool's own dependency-free validator with JSON-path issues). Exit 3 on mismatch with the issues on stderr; composes with `--output-format json` (the envelope gains `schema_valid`/`schema_issues`); refused with `--mode json` (exit 2 — an event stream has no single final answer). Print-mode exit contract now documented: 0 success, 1 error/empty, 2 usage, 3 schema-invalid. Verified live: valid answer -> `schema_valid true`, exit 0; prose answer -> exit 3 + "the final answer is not valid JSON". Tests: `headless-guards.test.ts` (18: turn semantics incl. the non-assistant-events pin, abort-once, no-ctx safety, fence stripping, schema paths, hostile-schema totality, renderer cap); the full self-learning suite 4353 pass / 0 fail. Docs: [usage](docs/usage.md) (the CI-flags paragraph + the exit-code contract), root + self-learning AGENTS.md contracts.
- **docs(DOX v3): the documentation tree reinstalled on the v3 framework — every AGENTS.md rebuilt from a fresh deep scan** — the existing DOX tree (installed 2026-10-15 on the June framework revision, since grown to 1,968 lines / 276 KB with v1-era local modifications — a 6-step Read Before Editing missing the v3 child-listing step, a 47 KB root carrying diary-entry prose, per-file test-count pins the tree had already outgrown) is removed and replaced wholesale with the **v3 framework** from [agent0ai/dox](https://github.com/agent0ai/dox) (the AGENTS.md file's third revision, 2026-09-01 — "Revise AGENTS.md for project indexing requirements"), whose bootstrap instruction is followed literally: *scan the project, build the DOX tree, go deep and create nested DOX files where needed*. **The root rail** carries the v3 framework text verbatim (Core Contract, the 7-step Read Before Editing with the parent-listed-child step, Update After Editing, Hierarchy, Child Doc Shape with the leave-it-empty rules, Style, Closeout) wrapped in freshly scanned project sections — Project Overview (service map, data flow, repo layout), Root Contracts (the bin entry chain, package.json surface, bun >= 1.4 floor, .env single-source rule, the 0.0.x version contract, the six-archive release contract, the landing truth rules for the three generated data files, the media never-delete rule, the .unown surface, the Vercel + verify-deploy gate) and Git Preflight (kept at its anchored URL — CONTRIBUTING.md links it); User Preferences keeps the Agnes endpoint assignments. **Every child doc rebuilt** to the v6-section shape (Purpose / Ownership / Local Contracts / Work Guidance / Verification / Child DOX Index) with content re-derived from a four-agent parallel deep scan of the actual tree, not carried over: `src/` (the landing app), `src/components/landing/` (1,142 lines → the nine-band structure, the three GENERATED files with their generator table, the honesty rules, the full verification ladder), `docs/` (all ~50 pages grouped by kind with the conventions and same-change sync rules), `mini-services/` + `mini-services/llm-bridge/` (routing, admission lanes, failover, degenerate guard, usage log), `pi-repo/packages/self-learning/` (the five mechanisms, terminal agent, engine API + M7 auth, sandbox, automations, env conventions — with the stale per-file test-count pins deliberately NOT restated), `scripts/` (lifecycle, builders, gates, generators, brand pipeline, the TUI capture lane), `blueprint/` (the frozen rule + v1→v2 mapping); `pi-repo/AGENTS.md` keeps its upstream pi-mono ruleset untouched with a rewritten Child DOX Index. **One NEW nested doc** per the v3 "go deep" instruction: `benchmarks/AGENTS.md` — the first DOX coverage for the 25+ suite tree (methodology contract, the exact six receipt paths that feed the landing page, the per-model canonical-run rule, the receipts-vs-scratch divide). Stale cross-references fixed in the same change: CONTRIBUTING.md's sync-map pointer (the map is gone — the sync rules live in the owning child docs now, per v3 Style "put broad rules in parent docs and concrete details in child docs"), and `docs/landing-research.md`'s pointer to the root sync map updated to its new home (Root Contracts) with the move recorded. The rebased tree keeps every 2026-10-24 round contract current in the fresh docs: the trust-round launch flags (`--permission-mode`, `--add-dir`), the flow-round resume + project-settings layer, and the CI-round headless guards (`--max-turns`, `--output-schema`, the 0/1/2/3 exit contract) are all in the root and self-learning contracts. The test fixture `scripts/tui-capture/fixtures/api-server/AGENTS.md` is NOT part of the DOX tree (the scripts doc now says so explicitly); no media assets touched. Verified: `node scripts/check_instruction_chars.mjs` (42 files, 0 findings — the new tree is invisible-Unicode clean), `bun run check:docs` (all 50 pages green), `bun run check:changelog` / `check:benchmarks` / `check:claims` green, `bun run typecheck` + `bun run lint` + `bun test src` (102) unchanged-green, production build + standalone smoke green.

### Fixed

- **resume tests (Windows): the fixture wrote invalid JSON — 4 phantom source failures on the release matrix, root-caused twice** — found by the v0.0.10 release matrix (the windows-latest verify leg failed 4 flow-round resume tests while ubuntu/macos passed, red on both release attempts). The real bug: `resume.test.ts`'s `writeSession` built the session-header line with a raw template interpolation of `opts.cwd` — on Windows that is `D:\a\Unown\...`, and `\a` is an INVALID JSON escape, so the "header" line never parsed. `parseHeader` correctly rejected it, every content-dependent scan came back empty (`checked 0 session file(s)`), and the four tests that require header/name reads failed while the filename-only paths (path pass-through, id-prefix with its basename fallback) kept working — exactly the split that made the bug invisible to the flow round's Linux-local verification. The fixture now serializes with `JSON.stringify` (as the real session writer always did), so the header is valid JSON on every platform. Corrections register, in the open: the first fix attempt (readHead/readTail falling back to `readFileSync` on zero positional reads) was built on a WRONG theory — that bun's positional `readSync` mis-handles `position: 0` on Windows — and changed nothing (run 2 failed identically; the fallback never fired; the reads were never broken). That change is fully reverted: the source was never at fault, and dead defense shipped on a false claim is worse than no defense. The `usage-cli` backward scanner (always `pos > 0`) was never affected either way. A SECOND Windows blocker surfaced once the four failures cleared (the matrix's third run: 4200 pass / 0 fail / 1 error): `headless-guards.test.ts`'s `loadOutputSchema` block called `mkdtempSync("/tmp/unown-schema-")` — the literal POSIX path does not exist on Windows (ENOENT, an unhandled describe-level error bun exits 1 on) — the CI-round test file landed after the v0.0.9 release and had never faced a Windows runner until this matrix. It now uses the suite's `TEST_TMP` redirect (the established cross-platform pattern); a sweep confirmed every other tmp consumer in the suite already uses platform tmp roots or pure string logic. Verified: the JSON-escape repro (raw interpolation -> "Invalid escape character a"; `JSON.stringify` -> parses, cwd round-trips), the resume + headless-guards suites 35/35, and the full self-learning suite 4353 tests 0 fail 0 error on Linux; the Windows proof is the release matrix itself (the 4 previously-failing tests plus the previously-erroring schema block are the regression pins).

## [0.0.9] - 2026-10-24

### Added

- **browser-subagent: browser automation as a first-class subagent + the live subagent-visuals round** — the browser surface shipped as a session tool, but the 2026 consensus (reverse-engineered from nine products — Claude Code, Codex CLI, Gemini CLI, Cursor, Amp, Playwright MCP, plus Warp/Goose/Aider/OpenCode/Crush passes; the record with per-product citations lives in [docs/browser-subagent-tui-research.md](docs/browser-subagent-tui-research.md)) is that browser capability belongs to a **subagent**: Gemini ships it ONLY as `browser_agent`, Cursor ships a built-in Browser subagent, and both say why — DOM snapshots, screenshots and console logs are context poison. **The `browser` builtin** (`task {agent: "browser"}`, sync or `async: true`): the child runs the work loop (start → navigate → snapshot → act on refs → verify) and returns a compact verified report under a fixed contract — `Outcome:` / `Did:` / `Pages:` / `Notes:` — while every snapshot, screenshot and console line stays in ITS context; the toolset is `browser` + `read/grep/find/ls` (pinned at the spawn level — the child's model requests carry exactly that allowlist, asserted in the E2E), lean routing (no MCP servers, no AGENTS.md injection — the child's tokens go to page snapshots), a model fallback chain like the worker's, and a **10-minute budget** (Gemini's `browser_agent` number; the 5-minute session default starved page flows). The prompt carries a **terminal-failure contract** (Gemini's list, adapted): browser-missing launch errors, `UNOWN_BROWSER_BLOCK` denials and repeated `net::ERR_*` on the same URL are reported verbatim with their remediation — policy and environment facts are never a retry loop; stale refs are NOT failures (snapshot and retry); and the child NEVER enters credentials the task did not explicitly provide (a login wall it cannot pass is `Outcome: partial` naming the wall). **The routing description says what NOT to delegate** (reading public pages is `webfetch`'s job) — the Gemini contract. **Per-agent timeouts** (the plumbing under the budget): a `timeoutMs` frontmatter field (numbers, `"10m"`, `"90s"` all parse; snake_case `timeout_ms`/`timeout` too) clamped to 30s–30min, a session-wide `UNOWN_SUBAGENT_TIMEOUT_MS` knob (human durations accepted, junk falls back — a typo can never zero the watchdog), per-agent values always win. **Live subagent visuals, both lanes**: the parent now derives a **humanized tool label** from the `tool_execution_start` args already streaming over the child's JSONL — `browser: navigate example.com`, `bash: npm test`, `read: src/foo.ts` (Gemini's `formatToolArgs` precedence; zero child-side changes, zero new events, zero polling) — so the sync task block reads `browser: ⟳ 3 turns, 2 tools in browser: navigate example.com · 12s` and the async fleet widget row reads `◉ 00:12 form-flow — running · browser: click [e12] · 3.4k tok`. Browser-active children (the browser agent, or any child currently driving the browser tool) get the **◉ glyph** in the fleet widget, distinguishable at a glance from repo work; fleet rows carry a **live token count**; finished sync rows carry their **wall-clock total** (`browser: ✓ 7 turns, 6 tools in 1m 13s`); and `/tasks` lists children with **full lifecycle metadata** — status, model, turns, tokens, current tool, elapsed, task preview — the number-one ask across Claude Code's subagent-row issues (#89844/#93324/#94872: rows missing model and effort read as black boxes). Also fixed on the way: the sync lane's tool counter listened for `tool_result_end`, an event the child never emits (the wire name is `tool_execution_end`) — every finished task row has shown "0 tools" since the lane shipped; both names now count. `UNOWN_BROWSER_NO_SANDBOX=1` is now actually read (the launch-retry error hint advertised it; nothing did) — it opts the FIRST launch into `--no-sandbox --disable-gpu` for containers/CI, where the retry path always forced them. Verified: 106 new pure tests (label humanization incl. URL compaction/token formatting/path shortening, timeout resolution incl. clamps + duration parsing + frontmatter plumb, the browser agent's contract, widget rendering with the ◉ glyph/fallback/tokens/width caps) + **4 spawn-level E2E with a real Chromium** against a multi-turn scripted mock bridge (the harness's first queue-driven mock — the flagship scenario drives the full delegation loop and asserts the child's report quotes the LIVE page's `submitted Ada/b` confirmation, which no mock could know; the allowlist, the missing-browser remediation and the blocklist denial are pinned end-to-end) + **as a real user**: `pty_capture_browsersub.py` drives the real TUI with the real Agnes model and a real headless Chromium through the whole visual surface — 6/6 audited stills (the task row, the humanized live label, the finished row with wall clock, the child report, the async ◉ fleet row, the /tasks metadata), landing in `public/landing/tui/browser-subagent/`; docs updated across [subagents](docs/subagents.md) (the built-in table, the frontmatter contract, the widget section, the delegation guide), [browser](docs/browser.md) (the delegation callout, the no-sandbox knob), [configuration](docs/configuration.md), `.env.example`, [usage](docs/usage.md) (the stale "30 actions" — it is 32) and the README. Also fixed on the release gate itself (found by the v0.0.9 3-OS verification, introduced by the same-day trust round): the redirect-exemption's lexical system-tree check compared only the literal spelling of the target, so on macOS — where `/var/tmp` is a symlink into `/private/var/tmp` and write roots are stored realpath'd — `--add-dir /var/tmp` then `> /var/tmp/log` was denied as an escape, denying exactly the root the user had added; the lexical layer now exempts the resolved path too (the resolved-path layer already did), and the `/opt story` test runs against a per-OS system-tree dir (POSIX `/var/tmp`, Windows `%SystemRoot%\Temp`) instead of a POSIX-only literal.

## [0.0.8] - 2026-10-24

### Added

- **parity: the 2026 consensus round — /btw, /output-style, /insights, session auto-titles, TUI turn-complete attention, cross-agent import** — six obvious-but-missing workflows discovered by reverse-engineering the shipped surfaces of ten coding agents (Claude Code, Codex, Antigravity, OpenCode, Crush, Amp, Goose, Aider, Cursor, Cline — four parallel research agents, every candidate gap cross-checked against Unown's actual command surface before a line was written; the already-covered list — queueing/steering, clipboard paste, context meter, palette, resume/fork, permissions/hooks, websearch, routines — was explicitly not rebuilt). **`/btw <question>`** (the Claude Code / Antigravity / OpenCode side-channel, ported): one bridge answer about the session rendered in a dismissible overlay — never entering the transcript, the receipts ledger or AutoDream's note mining; the context is the last 12 turns (text-only, per-message and total caps, the marker counted inside the cap); the answer dies with the overlay (Esc/Enter/q dismisses; print/RPC degrade to a notification block). **`/output-style [name]`** (Claude Code /output-style parity): four built-ins (default/concise/explanatory/proactive, real directives) plus custom `.unown/styles/*.md` (frontmatter name+description, body = directive; project styles win name collisions with user styles); the active style's directive rides the system prompt through `before_agent_start`, composed with the advertised-subagent catalog by appending both to the ONE base prompt (the duplication trap named and avoided); persists to `.unown/output-style.json` (project) over `~/.unown/agent/output-style.json` (user), `UNOWN_OUTPUT_STYLE` env wins. **`/insights [days]`** (Claude Code /insights parity — the self-improving agent's missing introspection): cross-session analytics over the user's OWN history — sessions and date range, message counts, top models, most-touched files (write/edit receipts, relativized), tool usage, notes (+ latest), token totals (prompt/completion/cached), the bridge window's per-model cost (honest `—` when no price is pinned), and friction (failed tool results + affected sessions); everything local, the days window bounds bridge costs only, every list capped with an honest `… +N more`. **Session auto-titles** (the every-competitor convention): once a turn settles in an interactive session, a cheap background bridge call names the session (3-6 words, ≤48 chars, shape-validated) so `/resume` is scannable — never overwrites a user-set `/name` (re-checked immediately before the write, the race guard), never runs in print/subagent/automation sessions, silent failure (a title must never cost a turn), `UNOWN_AUTO_TITLES=0` off. **TUI turn-complete attention** (`notify-tui.ts`): after an interactive turn that lasted at least `UNOWN_NOTIFY_MS` (shared with print mode — quick turns stay silent), a DEFERRED OSC-9 + BEL lands once the frame settles ("unown: done — <first 80 chars>") — the write is deferred 300ms so it never tears a render, a fresh turn cancels any pending ping (they never stack), and the gate family mirrors notify.ts exactly (`UNOWN_TUI_NOTIFY=0` TUI-specific, `UNOWN_NOTIFY=0` + `DO_NOT_TRACK=1` shared, TTY-only). **`unown sessions import <file> --from <auto|claude|codex>`** (the migration moat — Codex v0.140+ and Claude Code both ship /import): Claude Code project histories (`~/.claude/projects/<slug>/*.jsonl`) and Codex rollouts (`~/.codex/sessions/**/rollout-*.jsonl`) auto-detect (explicit `--from` forces) and convert to unown sessions — fresh uuids, strictly linear parent chain, tool_use↔toolCall and function_call↔toolCall mapping, tool_result→toolResult entries with toolCallId/isError, timestamps passed through — landing in `.unown/sessions/` where `/resume` and `sessions list` pick them up immediately; both argv orders handled. 189 new unit tests across six files (auto-title 38, output-style 35, import-convert 35, insights 17, btw 24, notify-tui 12+; the self-learning suite now 4200 green at the 43-error pre-existing tsc baseline). Live TUI verification (PTT-driven, `scripts/verify_features_live.py`): the style listing and set+persist, the /btw overlay answering from session context, the /insights report rendering against real session+bridge data, the auto-title landing in the session file ("Debug LLM bridge retry logic"), the OSC-9 ping emitted with `UNOWN_NOTIFY_MS=0`, and the model confirming it follows the active style directive — all PASS. Docs: [usage](docs/usage.md) (the command table + the auto-titles/attention paragraphs), [configuration](docs/configuration.md) (`UNOWN_TUI_NOTIFY`, `UNOWN_AUTO_TITLES`, `UNOWN_OUTPUT_STYLE`, the corrected `UNOWN_NOTIFY` row). The landing dex gains the twenty-second capability ("The parity round", honest pending stage, receipts straight at the six new modules).

## [0.0.7] - 2026-10-24

### Added

- **landing: the memory, browser and dream lanes — three filmed gaps closed, the hard way** — the dex's three flagship gaps flip from honest pending stages to audited reels, every lane a real end-to-end session against the live stack (bridge + engine, agnes-3.0-flash through the Agnes AI upstream, the ONNX bge-small embedder installed for real semantic recall — the first lanes captured with the post-swap memory platform). **memory-learn (№ 005 Memory)** — the catalog's first two-session story in one pane: teach the house rules + a real task, `/note` the lesson, `/quit`; AutoDream consolidates behind the scenes (the same `POST /api/dreams/run` the dashboard's dream-now key hits); a fresh session boots with the learned memory (footer chip v0 → v5 · 6 rules · dreamed just now), `/memory search` recalls it as a scored cross-scope result, and the closing task applies the lesson unprompted — the pane receipts "bun test run twice (per this repo's flake-guard convention) — 6/6 passing both times"; 6 stills + mp4/webm. **browser-verify (№ 011 Browser)** — the visitor check: `web navigate` opens the landing page on the local dev server, the a11y snapshot finds the exact curl install command, the GitHub link click lands on the real repo, screenshot + report; 3 stills + mp4/webm. **dream-dashboard (№ 008 Dream)** — `unown dashboard dreams` against the live engine: the real dream rows (done/gated/error, notes → merged · pruned · added), the dream detail with its canary/guard note and rendered output, and the Memory screen's four tiers + injection budget; 3 stills + mp4/webm. All lanes pass the full gate set (pane-truth + pixel + VLM still audits, frame scans, VLM video sampling); the dex contract test pins the new media mix (twelve movies, nine pending); the harness catalog (scenarios/index.js) grows to twelve specs with the two-session lane's `dreamUntilLearned` helper (fast 12s retries, 420s budget — the engine's own self-improvement loop holds the busy-mutex for multi-minute stretches). Verified: eslint, typecheck, `bun test src` (99), the claims/changelog/benchmarks/palette gates, build + standalone smoke, `playwright test` (7), `landing_shots` both schemes + reduced motion, and every lane's three-layer audit green.
- **commit: Unown-Session provenance trailers on every unown commit (the Amp thread-ID pattern)** — every `/commit` (and the opt-in `UNOWN_AUTO_COMMIT` snapshot) now closes with a trailer block: `Generated-by: unown` plus `Unown-Session: <session-id>` whenever the id is reachable through the extension context's read-only session manager. `git log` now leads straight back to the producing conversation: `git log --grep='Unown-Session: <id>'` finds every commit a session made; the id is the same one `/session` prints and `/blame --session` filters by — the receipts//blame ledger and the git history read one identifier end to end.
- **usage: the /usage in-session token & cost report (the Claude Code / Codex / Aider parity gap)** — the bridge usage report (CLI-only until now) is reachable from inside a session: `/usage [days] [--model <id>] [--json]` prints this session's rollup first (messages, in/out/cached tokens, models — the same attribution semantics `/session` uses, computed from the read-only entries), then the bridge's windowed per-model table + totals; a bare numeric arg is the days window (`/usage 7` — Claude Code's `d/w` ergonomics without the keybinding). Reuses the exact pure core the CLI `unown usage` runs (bridge aggregate when up, direct usage-log read when down), so behavior and pricing overrides match one-to-one; zero new fetch paths; `sessionUsageSummary` is a pure, defensive rollup (assistant usage + toolResult/compaction/branch-summary usage), exported for tests. Live TUI verified: session header + per-model bridge table rendered in a real session (agnes-3.0-flash, the cached-token split shown).
- **security: secret redaction on every path that leaves the machine (the Amp secret-redaction gap)** — a session transcript debugged with a real key must not carry that key into a gist or a transfer artifact: `/share` (vendored session-share.ts) redacts the JSONL Radius upload AND the gist HTML in place before any bytes leave the machine — known secret shapes (PEM blocks, `sk-` keys, `ghp_`/`github_pat_` PATs, AWS `AKIA` keys, Bearer tokens) become `[REDACTED:<type>]` markers, and the user is told how many were caught with `sha256[:16]` digests so they can identify what was redacted without the share carrying it; `unown sessions export` is redacted by default (a transfer artifact routinely changes hands) with `--raw` opting out for a lossless self-transfer between machines you own, the CLI reporting the redaction count; local `/export` is intentionally NOT redacted (your own disk, debugging value of exact content). The counting variant (`redactSecretsCounted`) lives in `adversary.ts` beside its existing twin table, the vendored share path carries its own documented twin copy (no cross-package import possible), markers identical. Functionally verified: a real CLI export of a session containing an `sk-` key + a `github_pat_` → 2 `[REDACTED:*]` markers, valid JSON, `--raw` keeps both verbatim.
- **landing: the category screens — a dex that grows on a band that does not** — the capability dex re-plumbed for a catalog that keeps growing: entry numbers are now DERIVED from array position (no hand-typed `no`; appending the twenty-second capability is a one-object edit), a four-category taxonomy lives in the data (`session` / `engine` / `workflows` / `configure`; a form's screen is its `type`, a behavior declares its own), a Gen-3 mode-select row of `aria-pressed` chips filters the list to the active screen's pool with derived counts and the cursor snapping to each pool's first filmed entry, the search intersects the pool, and every entry is addressable as `#dex-<id>` (a cold arrival selects it, lands on its screen and scrolls the band into view; walking the spine keeps the hash in step via `history.replaceState`, never `pushState`). The list column gained a ceiling (`lg:max-h-[65rem]`, the arithmetic that keeps list + column chrome under the pane's 76rem floor) so the band's height stops being a function of catalog size, and posters became live-pane attributes — only the shown pane mounts real `poster` attributes, hidden panes carry `data-poster` swapped in on show, which cut load-time media fetches from 10 to 3 (~800 KB; the cost no longer scales with the movie count) while every reel still mounts in the SSR DOM for crawlers and gates. The first axe run caught a WCAG 2.5.3 label-in-name failure in the chip row's `aria-label` overrides — the visible text is now the accessible name, the gloss riding as an sr-only tail. Ten new tests pin the derivation, the taxonomy partition, the selector contract, the screen snap, the search intersection, both deep-link behaviors and the poster attributes. Verified: eslint, typecheck, `bun test src` (94), the claims/changelog/benchmarks/palette gates, build + standalone smoke, `landing_audit --budget` (12.6vh / ≤3,200 words), `contrast_audit` (1,326 nodes, both schemes, zero AA failures), `theme_toggle_check`, `playwright test`, and a real-user browser pass over every screen, the keyboard surfaces, the deep links and 390px. Design record: [docs/landing-research.md](docs/landing-research.md) "the category screens"; contracts: [src/components/landing/AGENTS.md](src/components/landing/AGENTS.md).
- **landing: the search surface — canonical, sitemap, robots, one JSON-LD graph, a keyword-bearing h1** — everything a crawler or link-unfurler reads is now declared against one constant (`src/lib/site.ts`) and pinned by two new gates: a canonical link (absent before — every Vercel preview alias competed with the production origin), `og:url` / `og:site_name` / one `og:image` with explicit dimensions and alt (crawlers reliably use the first; the second image left the metadata), an explicit `summary_large_image` twitter card, robots meta directives that allow full previews (`max-image-preview: large`, `max-snippet: -1`), `robots.ts` replacing the static robots.txt (one rule group, the `Sitemap:` line derived — the old file's five groups were five copies of "allow everything"), `sitemap.ts` whose `lastmod` is the newest CUT release date read from the generated changelog stops (never build time — Google disregards an inflated lastmod — and never `[Unreleased]`, which is undated by design), and one JSON-LD `@graph`: `Organization`, `SoftwareApplication` (`offers.price: 0` as required for a free app, `applicationCategory: DeveloperApplication`, `softwareVersion` from package.json, no `aggregateRating` because none exists — a fabricated one is the self-review spam Google excludes) and `FAQPage` serialized from the FAQ data's own module (extracted to `faq-data.ts` so the accordion and the markup read one source and cannot drift), with `featureList` carrying the dex's own first practice line per entry. The h1 now carries the product category ("The terminal coding agent that remembers.") with the old headline preserved verbatim as the subhead's opening, so the deploy gate's text assertion survived; the description moved into the 70–160 band with the search phrases front-loaded. Verified: `e2e/seo.spec.ts` (new — head tags, robots.txt + sitemap.xml cross-referenced, the JSON-LD graph's required properties, one keyword-bearing h1, no skipped heading levels), the sitemap/robots route contracts pinned at the data level (`src/app/__tests__/seo-routes.test.ts`), `scripts/verify-deploy.mjs`'s new SEO section against the live deployment, and **Lighthouse on the production build at SEO 100 / Accessibility 100 / Best-Practices 100** (the standing contract). Design record: [docs/landing-research.md](docs/landing-research.md) "the search surface".

### Fixed

- **memory: the reconcile rationale leak — a model's "Reason:" sailed into project memory and broke the landing page's claims gate** — found live through the new memory-learn lane: the reconciler model answered an UPDATE op with its rationale inside the text field ("Verify engine health: … check this after any engine restart or config change. Reason: Tightened wording from 'restart' to broader 'health' verification since 'ok:true' is a general health indicator…") even though the op schema sends "why" to its own `reason` field — the leak rendered into this repository's own committed `.unown/memory/project.md` (via the browser-v2 round's sessions) and the landing loop band's verbatim-literal claims gate (`check:claims`) went red on main. Three-part fix: (1) `memzero.ts` strips a trailing `Reason: …` clause from every reconcile UPDATE text deterministically (the rationale was never memory), and the `RECONCILE_SYSTEM` prompt now states the `text` field is the complete new bullet only; pinned by a new unit test (`memzero-unit.test.ts` — the leak verbatim, stripped to the clean bullet); (2) the committed bullet is hand-curated back to its clean wording with an honest `changedBy: manual` provenance sidecar (v3); (3) the landing loop band's verbatim literal follows the cleaned bullet — `check:claims` green again (63/63 page literals resolve). Verified: `bun test src` (99), the self-learning memzero/dream/memory suites, the claims/changelog/benchmarks/palette gates, build + `playwright test` (7).
- **capture harness: the fresh-clone and reload-flash round — four fixes the three new lanes earned** — the lanes found four latent harness defects, all fixed and re-proven: (1) `prep_demo.sh` crashed on a fresh clone (`set -e` + a missing `.unown` backup the restore line required — a first-run user gets the TUI's own scaffold now, which is exactly what the boot screen should show); (2) `driver.js` now sets tmux `extended-keys on` at boot — a config-less server (the AppImage build, no `~/.tmux.conf`) filmed the TUI's "extended-keys is off" warning into every session-2 boot; (3) `assemble.py`'s near-blank frame filter is now near-UNIFORM in any luminance band — the G8 page-reload resync paints a solid gray frame (xterm's mid-attach state) that sailed past the dark-blank-only check into the dream-dashboard MP4 and failed the video VLM gate; a frame whose pixels sit ≥99.5% inside one 32-wide band is plumbing, not content; (4) scenario anchors corrected against the real surfaces — browser(v2) prints its tool blocks as `web <action>` (the display title, not the registered tool name), the dashboard dream detail's "consumed notes" section sits below the fold when the dream output is long (the `dream ·` heading is the stable anchor), and the second session's memory beat is `/memory search` (the scored cross-scope recall fits the pane; `/learning`'s full stack print scrolls the PROJECT tier out of the 36-row viewport). Verified: every lane re-run clean through the full gate set.

## [0.0.6] - 2026-10-24

### Added

- **browser(v2): the stealth + CAPTCHA tiers — CDP-signal silence, coherent fingerprints, humanized input, and the solve pipeline** — v1 could drive the web but not survive it: the Runtime domain was enabled on every page (the 2026-generation bot check — a page hooking `Error.prepareStackTrace` around `console.debug` — fires on exactly that), the headless UA still said `HeadlessChrome`, the screen stayed 800x600, and a CAPTCHA was a dead end. The v2 round adds two tiers to the same closed-graph CDP stack, zero new dependencies. **Stealth** (opt-in: `browser start stealth=true` or `UNOWN_BROWSER_STEALTH=1`): the Runtime domain is *never* enabled and every internal page op (snapshots, element prep, fills, detection, injection) runs in an isolated world (`Page.createIsolatedWorld`); the fingerprint is applied by the browser itself over the protocol — `Network.setUserAgentOverride` with full `userAgentMetadata` (UA, `navigator.platform`, `navigator.userAgentData` all agree; the claimed Chrome version *is* the launched engine's, read from `--version`), `Emulation.setLocale/TimezoneOverride`, and `setDeviceMetricsOverride` with `screenWidth/screenHeight` (kills the 800x600 default) — no page-JS patching, no init scripts (the 2026 consensus: injected lies are themselves detectable); identities per OS (`UNOWN_BROWSER_OS` windows/macos/linux, coherent screen/viewport/locale/timezone, invalid IANA ids fall back rather than silently no-op); headless-shell binaries excluded from stealth discovery; humanized input (seeded-PRNG bezier mouse paths from the cursor's last position, per-character typing with jittered delays — `UNOWN_BROWSER_HUMANIZE=0` off, `_SEED` reproducible); proxy passthrough (`UNOWN_BROWSER_PROXY` → `--proxy-server` + loopback bypass); honest degradation (console capture is impossible without the Runtime enable — Log-domain only, stated in results); `stealth status|verify` as first-class actions reading the live fingerprint across ten axes. **CAPTCHAs** (`browser captcha detect|solve`, auto-detect on every navigate, auto-solve with `UNOWN_CAPTCHA_AUTO=1`): the detector classifies Turnstile (widget + full-page interstitial), reCAPTCHA v2/v3, hCaptcha, DataDome, AWS WAF, GeeTest, Arkose and image captchas from the isolated world; the solve ladder runs a native input-level checkbox click first (trusted events cross iframes), then 2Captcha/CapSolver (clients with injectable transports; tokens injected through native setters + events, React-safe), then vision OCR on the repo's own Agnes preset (`agnes-3.0-flash` reads the clipped captcha image — never the page; verified live: it read a four-character fixture and typed the answer), and reports the vendor gaps instead of faking them — **hCaptcha was dropped by every major solving service as of 2026-10** (2Captcha's page is a 404), so it gets the native click and an honest "not solvable" verdict. Every claim was measured against Chrome for Testing 143 *before* production code was written (`scripts-dev/dbg-stealth-probe*.ts`, the raw receipts): the classic `error.stack` trap is dead in 143, the modern `prepareStackTrace` detector FIRES with `Runtime.enable` and stays SILENT without it; `Log.entryAdded` carries no console messages and `Runtime.bindingCalled` delivers no events un-enabled; isolated worlds see the DOM but not page JS; the override stack lands coherently on https pages; `--window-size` does not fix the headless screen but the screen params do. Also fixed on the way: a profile-lock race (two concurrent sessions can win the default profile's SingletonLock between check and launch — the startup retry now uses a fresh temp profile, never the still-locked one). Verified: 31 new unit tests (identity/UA-CH coherence, launch args, humanize determinism under a fixed seed, both solver wire protocols against mocked transports, the vision request shape, discovery's headless-shell filter) + 10 new live tests (the detector in **both directions**, the 10-axis verify, in-page-counted humanized mouse paths, five detection fixtures, token injection, env-override coherence) + the live vision call against the real Agnes endpoint + **as a real user**: a headless agent session on the Agnes model drove a stealth browser through `bot.sannysoft.com` and reported **zero FAIL rows**, with `stealth verify` at 10/10. Design record + the D17–D27 decision table: [docs/browser-research.md](docs/browser-research.md); user-facing page with the two tiers, the workflows and every env knob: [docs/browser.md](docs/browser.md).

### Fixed

- **security: the supply-chain and static-analysis round — every Dependabot and CodeQL code-scanning alert cleared, with behavior equivalence machine-verified for every rewritten parser** — four audited changesets on 2026-10-23. (1) *Actions pinning*: all 33 action references re-pinned to SHA-verified current versions (`actions/checkout` 11d5960 → 3d3c42e # v7.0.1, `actions/attest-build-provenance` e8998f9 → 4d10147 # v4.2.2) and the last floating tags in the probe workflows pinned (`setup-node` v4.4.0, `upload-artifact` v4.6.2) — superseding Dependabot PRs #7/#9, closed. (2) *npm advisories*: the vendored `pi-repo/` tree bumped at the lockfile level — undici 6.28.0 → 6.29.1, vite 8.0.16 → 8.3.5, vitest + @vitest/mocker 4.1.11 → 4.1.12 (nine workspaces, stale root 4.1.9 entries pruned, the mem0ai subtree the lockfile had been missing synced in), shell-quote 1.10.0 → 1.12.0, source-map-js 1.2.1 → 1.2.2, brace-expansion 5.0.9 → 5.0.12, plus `@earendil-works/gondolin` 0.12.0 → 0.13.0 in both the root and example lockfiles (API surface diffed against the 0.12/0.13/0.14 tarballs before upgrading; node engine comment updated). 0.14.0 — which drops node-forge for a `node:crypto` DER builder — is hard-blocked by this repo's `min-release-age=2` policy until 2026-10-24 and is the documented follow-up; the two node-forge advisories (GHSA-86w9-cpqp-85rv, no patched release exists upstream) are dismissed `fix_started` with that plan. `install-lock/` and the shrinkwrap regenerated through the repo's own scripts; the pre-existing red `check:pinned-deps` gate fixed (self-learning's `better-sqlite3`/`mem0ai` now exact-pinned). The example sub-lockfiles needed `--workspaces=false` (npm refuses subset installs inside the workspace tree). Dependabot: 34 open → **0**. (3) *CodeQL, first pass* (38 of 50 alerts auto-closed on rescan): the `baseUrl.includes("api.openai.com")` provider-detection family — including the unflagged siblings — converted to a shared parse-then-compare helper (`urlHostMatches`: hostname equality or leading-dot suffix, fail-closed; verified against all 1548 catalog baseUrls plus the `evil.net/x/api.openai.com` / `@evil.net` / `.evil.net` bypass family); polynomial regexes rewritten as linear equivalents with machine-verified equivalence — trailing-separator strips via the `(^|[^X])X+$` form, cron's range split by `indexOf`, the rm-flag runs with non-overlapping classes, `parseNpmSpec`'s dead `(?:\\/[^@]+)?` group dropped (200k-random-input proof), `parseSkillBlock`/`parseDiffLine` as manual scans reproducing the old lazy/greedy/backtrack capture semantics exactly; the openrouter OAuth callback HTML-escapes every request-derived interpolation; engine-supervisor's win32 detach quote-wraps the executable (the SS64-correct `start "" "exe"` form) and refuses cmd metacharacters in it; `stdioSpawnSpec` likewise refuses metacharacters in the command token; websearch's tag strips loop until stable and decode `&amp;` last; systemd ExecStart quoting escapes backslash first; import session ids come from `node:crypto`; API errors surface `.message` only with stacks logged server-side; git remotes/refs scheme-validated at install/update boundaries. (4) *CodeQL, second pass* (the survivors + two new findings on the just-landed browser package): `substituteArgs` is now a manual linear scanner (the `([^}]*)\\}` complement-class shape was still quadratic on unterminated `${` runs; 94/94 tests green), the memzero header filters are manual linear checks, `</script foo=…>`-style end tags tolerated, the package-manager git flows restructured to the canonical sanitizer shapes (`safeGitRemote` returns the remote from inside the positive prefix branches, `safeGitRef` carries the explicit `--` check, `clone`/`fetch` argv gain literal `--` separators — git-verified), the remaining `String(err?.message ?? err)` taint sources (llm, hooks-config, localml, gittools, runner, review, pr-review, browser session) cut with the `instanceof Error ? .message : String()` barrier, and the browser `storage` eval passes its payload through `JSON.parse` of a string literal — a `__proto__` key in the old object-literal form set the prototype. Two `stdioSpawnSpec` env-injection findings assessed false positives and dismissed with the five-layer defense cited. CodeScanning: 50 open → **0 open, 0 dismissed-but-fixable** (2 documented FPs). Verification: self-learning suite 3923/3923 (browser tests included), coding-agent failure set byte-identical to the pre-change baseline (27 pre-existing model-catalog-drift failures), agent 418, tui latex 110 + markdown 81, ai identical to baseline, tsc error counts identical to HEAD at every step; every equivalence claim backed by an exhaustive or randomized harness (`scripts/*-proof*.mjs`).

## [0.0.5] - 2026-10-23

### Added

- **browser automation: the `browser` session tool — drive a real Chromium over raw CDP, zero new runtime dependencies** — the terminal agent could read the web (`web_search`/`webfetch`) but never touch it: no JS execution, no forms, no login sessions, no visual verification. Now every session registers a `browser` tool that speaks the Chrome DevTools Protocol directly over Bun's native WebSocket (`pi-repo/packages/self-learning/src/browser/` — session/cdp/a11y/config/tools, ~3.6k lines), so the compiled binaries' dependency graph stays closed. One tool, one action enum (30 actions): navigate/back/forward/reload, a11y-tree `snapshot` with `[eN]` refs (400 elements / 60k chars, truncation noted), `find` (grep the snapshot), `text`/`html` reads, `click`/`hover` by **ref / CSS selector / x-y coordinates** (the vision tier), trusted input via `Input.dispatchMouseEvent`/`dispatchKeyEvent` at the element center after scrollIntoView (`isTrusted` checks pass — measured), `type` (+Enter submit, clear), `press_key` (combos, mac spellings), `fill_form` (many fields, one call), `select_option`, `scroll`, `drag`, `screenshot` (returns an **image content block the model sees directly** — the vision loop: screenshot, ground x/y from the picture, coordinate-click; JPEG default, element clips, fullPage), `eval` (async ok), `tabs` (list/new/select/close), bounded `wait` (text / textGone / url / download / ≤30s sleep — deliberately no network-idle), `resize`, `console`, `requests`, `cookies` (list/set/clear), `upload` (jailed to project root + system tmp, like `view_image`), `pdf`, `storage` (save/load cookies + current-origin localStorage). Auto-starts headless on first use (`UNOWN_BROWSER_HEADLESS=0` to watch), browser discovery chain `UNOWN_BROWSER_PATH` → per-OS system Chrome/Chromium/Edge → Playwright cache (wildcard dirs, newest-first; literal Windows backslash paths, not `path.join` on the host) → an actionable error naming both install paths; attach to a running browser with `UNOWN_BROWSER_CDP` (ws/http/host:port, `/json/version` discovery included — the already-logged-in Chrome, never killed on teardown); persistent profile at `~/.unown/browser/profile` with automatic temp-profile fallback when locked (two concurrent sessions never fight); `UNOWN_BROWSER_BLOCK` host-glob deny list checked before every navigation (localhost stays allowed — driving the dev server is a first-class journey); downloads land in the session cwd with the exact path reported, `wait {download:true}` rides out Chrome's `.crdownload` rename; honest fingerprints (never `--enable-automation`, drops `AutomationControlled` — no stealth theater); graceful teardown (`Browser.close` + SIGTERM/SIGKILL fallback + exit hooks; a crashed session leaks no zombie Chromium). Every mutation returns a fresh a11y snapshot so the next decision is grounded (Playwright MCP's contract); stale refs are an actionable error, not a silent misclick. Design reverse-engineered from **seven shipping products** (Browser Use 0.13, Stagehand v4, Playwright MCP 0.0.83, Skyvern, Anthropic's `browser_toolset_20260801`, Browserbase/Steel, vercel-labs agent-browser) — tool surfaces, element models and session semantics read from their docs, `llms.txt`, MCP listings and SDK types; the record and the D1-D16 decision table live in [docs/browser-research.md](docs/browser-research.md), the user-facing page in [docs/browser.md](docs/browser.md). Verified: 20 unit tests (discovery per-OS with injectable fs, URL normalization incl. `localhost:3000/x`, host-glob gating, snapshot formatting, key parsing) + 31 live tests driving real Chromium through the module's own discovery (fixture site with iframes, open shadow DOM, dialogs, downloads, upload jail positive + rejection, storage round-trip, PDF magic bytes) + **as a real user**: headless `unown -p` sessions against the live web — example.com (snapshot + vision-described screenshot), news.ycombinator.com (click, type, submit, extract ranked results, screenshot described down to the result count) — receipts in `.unown/receipts/`.

### Fixed

- **landing: the defect round — a real hydration failure, the square-corner and type contracts, and three numbers that were not checkable** — a full-page audit (code-vs-contract, the repo's own gates, and a browser pass at 1440/1024/390 in both schemes and reduced motion) found and fixed: (a) **reduced-motion visitors got a full hydration failure** — framer's `useReducedMotion()` returns the live media-query value during hydration, so `FadeUp`, `BarRow`, `LiftBar` and the dex selection readout rendered a different first client tree than the server (`translateY(20px)` vs `none`, "playing" vs "poster") and React discarded the server render entirely; the new `useReducedMotionAfterMount()` pins the first client render to the server's assumption and adopts the real value after mount (zero console/page errors in both motion passes, was 3/3 failures); (b) **five `rounded-*` utilities on a page whose rule is square corners** (skip link, CopyButton, the hero/loop/integrity dots, `.link-sweep`'s focus ring); (c) **the proof stat band's four headline figures set in Pixelify Sans**, where `57` reads `S7` — now Geist Mono; (d) the **hero seal's ghosted base state** leaked into Firefox (`opacity: .25` unconditionally, the scroll-timeline reveal living behind `@supports`) — base state is the finished word, dead `.seal`/`seal-breathe` and `.unown-breathe`/`.unown-sweep` rules removed, seal reveal + halo gated under `prefers-reduced-motion`; (e) the **SWE-bench caveat's Integrity link could not reach the Integrity pane** (the zero lives behind a `hidden` tab) — `#proof-tab-integrity` now activates the tab on arrival and on click; (f) **band seams doubled** where five `border-y` sections stacked against their neighbour's `border-t` — every band carries `border-t` only and showcase's outlier padding joins the others; (g) **nav labels wrapped to two lines at 1024–1079px** inside the 64px bar — single-line at the `lg` floor now (12.5px, `whitespace-nowrap`, full size from 1100px), the `self-improving` chip moved onto Press Start 2P's 8px native grid from 7px; (h) **the dex band's height swung ~195px per selection** against its own constant-height contract — a `lg:min-h` panel floor sized to the tallest entry with the receipts row pinned to the panel edge makes all twenty entries render the band at the same measured height (1740px at 1440, was 1542–1737); (i) the dex game-voice entry set in `.quote`+`<blockquote>` mislabeled the page's own voice as a quotation — it is `gb-term` on the `.gb-box` now; (j) the loop beats terminal clipped its gated metadata line 68px at 1440 (wraps now, per the terminal-column contract), the install one-liner overflowed 320–390px viewports (fits below 430px), showcase screen tiles ellipsized 76px of caption at 390 (wraps), hero expander arrows `\u25B8`/`\u2192` fell outside the fonts' latin subset into OS fallbacks (drawn `PixelCursor`/`MoveRight` now); (k) **three numbers were not checkable, on the page whose argument is checkability**: the star-count fallback printed a fabricated 128 when GitHub is unreachable (it is the repo's real count, 0), lexicon `U` said "9 providers" against the model band's ten tiles, and the vendored-marks header named the wrong text-only tiles (OpenAI, Agnes AI, llama.cpp — DashScope carries the Alibaba Cloud mark). Docs follow the code: the landing AGENTS.md's hydration/reduced-motion, provider-mark, `.quote`, dex-constancy and VLM-lane sections, the generated-data headers (`landing_claims.mjs` no longer cites the deleted `origin.tsx`/`mechanisms.tsx`), `globals.css`'s contrast table re-synced to the live gate output, and the stale `2.1.0` reference in `changelog.test.tsx`. Verified: 83/83 unit tests, eslint + tsc clean, the palette/claims/changelog/benchmarks gates, the scroll budget (12.9vh / 3023w), the a11y functional checks (deep link + accordion), and zero console errors in both motion modes.
- **the DOX tree was two upstreams behind — every stale documentation surface caught up in one round** — the 2026-10-23 re-walk of the documentation hierarchy (the same audit that found the model-spec gap) verified six findings against the tree and fixed all of them: (a) `docs/AGENTS.md`'s ownership list named 25 of 46 pages — the 21 unowned pages (the command manuals acp/custom-commands/ralph/recipes/repomap/repo-wiki/routines/standing-orders/usage-tracking/watch/web-results-store, the UX records permissions/split-view/tui-*-plan ×4, the engine records detached-engine-architecture/-research, and the roadmaps sdlc-feature-roadmap/agentic-asset-workflow) are now owned, grouped by kind, and the bogus root-relative `src/sandbox.ts` in the sync-list is spelled `pi-repo/packages/self-learning/src/sandbox.ts`; (b) the root AGENTS.md `bin/unown` contract documented 15 commands while the CLI dispatches 25 — the sixteen missing surfaces (`providers`, `mcp`, `init-agents`, `doctor-instructions`, `engine`, `routines`, `watch`, `repomap`, `ralph`, `usage`, `webcache`, `sessions`, `blame`, `recipes`, `wiki`, `acp`) are now in the contract with their HELP grammar, and the setup preset list gains `agnes` (a real preset since 2026-10-19, verified against `presets.ts`); (c) `src/AGENTS.md`'s Child DOX Index still listed the deleted `src/lib/AGENTS.md` — the dangling entry is gone and `src/lib/` is covered by the parent doc; (d) the README Documentation table gains the six rows it was missing (landing-research, testing-strategy, benchmark-research, sdlc-feature-roadmap, agentic-asset-workflow, tui-beautification-plan — 46 of 46 pages now listed); (e) CONTRIBUTING.md still called `src/` "dashboard" and e2e "against a mock engine" — both rewritten to the landing page and the production standalone build (plus the three same-family wordings at lines 28/48/72). `check_docs` green over all 47 pages; the only genuinely broken reference in the corpus was (c), everything else resolved.
- **landing: the memory-lift chart's axis was typed at 125% and the agnes-3.0-flash receipt spent +197.1% — the warm bar painted 237.7% of the track, across the token label, the panel border and the page** — the chart's shared domain was a hardcoded `LIFT_SCALE = 125` chosen when the worst warm run was +23.2%, and `agnes-3.0-flash`'s 319,659-token warm run against its 107,583-token cold one rendered at `(319659 / 107583) × 80% = 237.7%` of the track width in an unclipped absolutely-positioned element: the amber bar crossed its own token count, the card border and the viewport edge, and the chart's caption still promised the scale exists "so the models that spent more are not cropped". Bar length encodes the data, so the axis extends — the bar is never clamped, cropped or log-scaled: `LIFT_SCALE` is now derived from the receipts (the smallest multiple of 25 covering every warm run's share of its cold run, floored at 125 so the axis always runs past the baseline — 300 for the current receipts, back to 125 if a future refresh brings the data in range), the warm baseline tick tracks `COLD_BAR_W` instead of a hardcoded `left-[80%]` that went stale the moment the domain moved, and the caption prints the derived scale. `LIFT_SCALE`/`COLD_BAR_W` are exported and pinned by `__tests__/benchmarks-lift.test.ts` (axis ≥ 125, the exact derivation against the receipts, cold/tick sharing one number, and the spill regression: every cold and warm bar ≤ 100% of the track). The landing AGENTS.md's generated-data section carries the rule. Verified at 1440 and 390 in both schemes on the dev server: the agnes warm bar now ends at 99% of the track, inside the panel border, tick on the cold baseline.

- **browser: the persistent profile honors the empty-string env convention** — `defaultProfileDir` resolved `$UNOWN_HOME` with `??`, but the repo-root `.env` ships `UNOWN_HOME=` EMPTY (bun auto-loads it for every `bin/unown` run), so an empty-but-set value made the profile land at `<repo>/browser/profile` instead of `~/.unown/browser/profile` — found exactly that way: a stray `browser/profile/` directory in the working tree after the first live sessions. Now `||`, the documented repo convention ("an empty string must mean unset"), pinned by the unit suite; logins persist under the real unown home.
- **browser: Enter submits forms — a keyDown without its char event runs no default action** — found live, the way it ships: the Hacker News journey (`type` + `submit:true`) typed fine but never navigated — the search form simply did nothing. Isolated with a variant probe against the live form: `rawKeyDown` + `keyUp` → nothing; `keyDown` carrying `text: "\r"` → submits. Blink runs a key's default action (form submit on Enter, button activation on Space) only when the keyDown carries the char event, and `parseKeyCombo` minted named keys without one. Enter now carries `text: "\r"`, Space carries `text: " "` (pinned in the unit suite), and the live suite grew the real regression — `type {submit: true}` on the fixture form must fire `onsubmit` (the old test asserted only that the typed text landed in the input; the submit path was never exercised against an actual form).

- **the code's no-env defaults still said Dashscope/kimi while the docs, the preset and the live wiring said Agnes** — `.env.example` (the template every new install authors from) still shipped `BRIDGE_UPSTREAM_URL=https://coding-intl.dashscope.aliyuncs.com/v1`, `BRIDGE_MODEL=kimi-k2.5` and the kimi/qwen allow-list, and the same stale trio lived in six code fallbacks: the bridge's `loadConfig` (pure.ts — `BRIDGE_MODEL`/`BRIDGE_UPSTREAM_URL`/`BRIDGE_KNOWN_MODELS` defaults), the engine's model registration (`llm.ts` `PI_MODEL_ID ||`), the terminal first-run seed (`term.ts` `bridgeKnownModels` + the modelId chain) and the hooks callLlm fallback (`termhooks.ts`). A fresh clone with no `.env` edits would have seeded a models.json whose default model its own upstream does not serve. All six sites now default to the Agnes pair (`agnes-3.0-flash` / `agnes-2.5-flash` / `https://apihub.agnes-ai.com/v1`), matching the documented default and the shipped preset; the `dashscope` PRESET keeps its own qwen default (it describes its own provider). Pinned by the byte-identical `loadConfig({})` defaults test and the empty-env fallback test (pure.test.ts), the fresh-seed catalog test and the no-env helper test (models-catalog.test.ts), and the bridge-preset registration test (providers-cli.test.ts — the served allow-list assertion now pins the agnes pair). Docs follow in the same change: `.env.example`, `docs/configuration.md`'s defaults table, `docs/BEHAVIOR.md`'s default-models card, the README architecture diagram + example `.env` block, and the llm-bridge/mini-services AGENTS.md env contracts.

- **landing: the capability dex grows an eleventh form — Browser, the window creature** — the bestiary's first new specimen since the v3 atlas: a living browser window (rounded frame in the page's own plate ramp, chrome band with three window dots, a bright rendered-page plate carrying the face, stubby feet) whose one detached part is the story itself — a solid gold cursor, because the trusted click is the feature. Authored in the atlas's drawing kit (mass before parts, details interior, one deliberate glint), all sprite gates green (44x41 bbox at baseline row 52, 8 colors, 16% dark, edge/orphan clean), reviewed by the vision model against the full 11-sprite contact sheet — "matches the established scale, outline thickness and pixel density… a natural addition rather than a disruption" (plus two polish rounds it asked for: the cursor solidified, the noise-bar text lines removed). The dex is now twenty-one entries: forms № 001-011, behaviors renumbered № 012-021, every count pin in the test suites moved with it.

### Agent Behavior

- **the built-in agents' model fields were Dashscope relics — two no-op overrides dropped, one fake fallback chain repaired** — `scout` and `background-explore` both hard-coded `model: "qwen3.7-plus"`, an id that only exists on the Agnes upstream via bridge routing (unknown id → `BRIDGE_ROUTE_DEFAULT` → the same `agnes-3.0-flash` the child would resolve anyway) — so the override selected nothing, while the child's own models.json (seeded from the allow-list) does not register the id at all. Both built-ins drop the field and keep the levers that still buy something (`effort: low`, `mcpServers: []`, `injectAgentsMd: false`); their descriptions no longer claim a cheaper model. `worker`'s fallback chain (`qwen3.7-plus, qwen3.6-plus`) was worse than useless: every chain member routes onto the SAME default model, so a provider-style failure of the primary rotated to a re-request of the identical endpoint model — the chain is now `agnes-2.5-flash`, the one actually-served alternate, so the rotation changes something real. Pinned by the updated built-ins tests (scout's model is `undefined` — the pin now documents the absence; background-explore's pin likewise) and the routing catalog suite. `docs/subagents.md`'s routing table, resolution-order prose, fallback examples and the shipped-example paragraph updated in the same change, plus `docs/BEHAVIOR.md`'s per-subagent routing paragraph. Full suite: self-learning 3939 tests / 0 fail.

- every terminal session now registers **sixteen** unown tools (was fifteen): the new `browser` tool (auto-start headless Chromium, snapshot+refs, trusted input, vision screenshots, launch-or-attach, persistent profile, deny list — full card in [docs/BEHAVIOR.md](docs/BEHAVIOR.md) and [docs/browser.md](docs/browser.md)). Read-only plan mode and unattended read-only routine runs do **not** gain it (it types, clicks and submits — not a read-only surface); subagents get their own browser per process. BEHAVIOR.md's tool registry, usage.md's session-tool table, configuration.md's env reference, `.env.example`, the README feature list and its Documentation table all moved in the same change.

### Changed (the landing page — the benchmarks pane's density pass, 2026-10-23)

- **landing: the benchmarks pane lost ~9% of its height and kept every figure, receipt link and caveat** — the pane was the proof band's tallest block (2.74vh of the page's 12.7, its memory-lift panel alone 735px at 1440), so the "shorten the benchmarks" round applied the researched density patterns (grouped-bar comparisons, condensed-table row density, no legend/label duplication, tabular numerals right-aligned) without cutting a single detail: the memory-lift chart's two stacked labelled bar rows per model became **one grouped cold/warm pair on a shared track** (the standard two-series grouped-bar pattern — the legend already names the series, so the per-bar `cold`/`warm` labels were duplication; the 100%-of-cold baseline tick now spans the pair at `COLD_BAR_W`, and both token counts read as one `cold → warm` right-aligned `tabular-nums` figure, the same arrow the time chips use; each bar keeps a `role="img"` aria-label, `LIFT_SCALE`/`COLD_BAR_W` and the spill regression test are untouched); paddings and margins dropped one step on the 4px grid (stat band `py-6`→`py-5`, cards and panels `p-5`→`p-4`, inter-block `mt-6`→`mt-5`); and the regression suite's three `gb-inset` boxes became three divide-y table rows (reference data reads as a table, not as card chrome). Nothing was removed: all six tracks still render, the `no_code` counts, the easy/hard splits, the head-to-head box, the negative-controls box, the not-measured caveat and every receipt link survive verbatim. Measured: the pane 2,019 → 1,840px, the proof band 2,466 → 2,287px (2.74 → 2.54vh), the page 12.7vh / 3,051w → 12.5vh / 3,047w after the rail regen, zero horizontal overflow and zero console errors at 1440 and 390. The AGENTS.md width-tween note now names `LiftPair`. Verified: 87/87 unit tests, tsc + eslint clean, the claims/benchmarks/palette/changelog gates, e2e 3/3; a geometry probe in the light scheme pins the agnes warm bar 8.4px inside its track with a 12px gap to the token readout (refuting the VLM light-pass's two structural FAILs — its third FAIL, the negative-controls box's density, is the untouched intentional fine-print design, consistent with prior rounds); dark-scheme VLM pass 5/5.

## [0.0.4] - 2026-10-23

### Added

- **landing: a section menu below `lg` — the phone visitor gets the seven anchors back** — below the `lg` breakpoint the header carried no navigation at all: `NAV_LINKS` was `hidden lg:flex`, so a 390px visitor's only in-page navigation was the footer at the bottom of a 13-viewport page. The bar measured exactly full at 390px (wordmark 107px + actions 227px + padding = 390, zero slack), so the fix is a disclosure menu rather than cramming: a pixel-legal three-bar button (`gb-btn-dark`, `crispEdges` SVG like every other chrome glyph — a rounded lucide `Menu` would be the one smooth vector on a page of square ones) opens a panel that overlays the page from the sticky header (`absolute inset-x-0 top-full`, solid `bg-panel`, the same gold rule, `max-h-[calc(100dvh-4rem)]` overflow for landscape phones). The panel carries the two header actions that no longer fit the bar below `lg` — the gold **Get started** and the GitHub mark, led by the CTA — then the seven anchors, mapped over the same `NAV_LINKS` array the desktop nav renders (one source of truth, no third list to drift). WAI-ARIA disclosure-navigation pattern, not a menu widget: `aria-expanded` + `aria-controls` on the button, the panel is always in the DOM and toggled through the `hidden` attribute (so `aria-controls` always resolves and a closed panel is out of the tab order and the a11y tree), focus stays on the button on open, **Esc closes from anywhere**, focus leaving the header closes (tabbing past the last link, or tapping the page behind the panel — the panel can never orphan itself open over content the visitor scrolled to), and every link click closes as it navigates. The desktop bar (≥ `lg`) is byte-identical: same links, same actions, same hover cursor. Verified as a real user against the production build: at 390px the menu opens with all seven anchors + both actions, a FAQ tap lands at `#faq` with the section top exactly at the 64px `scroll-mt-16` line and the panel closed; no horizontal overflow at 375/390/768/1023; both axe passes, the contrast audit (both schemes), the budget audit (12.8vh — the menu is below-lg and costs the measured page nothing), the theme-toggle check and the palette gate stay green; a VLM pass over dark and light 390px screenshots found no overlap or cutoff. Tests: `landing-copy.test.tsx` +2 (the closed-disclosure contract — panel present, `hidden`, out of the tree; opens with every anchor + both actions; closes on link click and on Escape), and the composition test's anchor-resolution sweep covers the new links for free because they come from `NAV_LINKS`.
- **Routines v5 — reliability: failed-run retries, chained routines, engine hygiene (env hot-reload, log rotation)** — the two questions an unattended schedule eventually faces, answered with opt-in knobs (unattended runs spend tokens, so neither is ever a default). **`--retry-max N`** (0..10, default 0 = off) re-runs a FAILED occurrence up to N more times with k8s `backoffLimit` semantics: **`--retry-backoff <sec>`** (30..3600, default 300) waits before the first retry, later attempts double it capped at 1 h; retries land in history as their own runs (trigger `retry`, `attempt 2+`, linked by `retry_of`) and **never consume a slot or the `--max-runs` budget** — the occurrence was counted once, at its original slot; a success of ANY trigger kind (a manual `run`-now included) disarms the pending chain; at most one chain per routine, tracking the most recent failure; a failed once-kind HOLDS lifecycle `active` (only the retry can fire) until the chain resolves;
